- **next**: Use when the developer starts a Hashsmith session, asks what to do next or what is on the board, or runs /hashsmith:next. The one entry point that reads the board and proposes the next step. ([Hashlogics/hashsmith](https://github.com/Hashlogics/hashsmith/tree/HEAD/skills/next))
